A: "A vehicle that uses hydrogen as its onboard fuel for motive power is considered a hydrogen vehicle, not a flex-fuel or bi-fuel vehicle. A: "Season of Migration to the North is a counter-narrative to Heart of Darkness, which was written by the Polish-British novelist Joseph Conrad.
